   34: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
   35:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   36:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
   37:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
   38: 
   39:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
   40: 
   41:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
   42: 
   43:   return list
   44:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
   45:     .map((draw) => {
   46:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   47:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   48:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
   49:     })
   50:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
   51:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
   52: }

   57: 
   58: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
   59:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
   60:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
   61: 
   62:   const n = Number(s);
   63:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
   64:   if (n === 0) return 25;
   65: 
   66:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
   67:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
   68: }
   69: 
   70: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   71:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   72:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   73:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   74: 
   75:   let score = 0;
   76:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   77:   if (h) score += 20;
   78:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   79: 
   80:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   81:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   82: 
   83:   return score;
   84: }
   85: 
   86: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
   87:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
   88:     ? Number(p.position)
   89:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
   90:       ? Number(p.posicao)
   91:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
   92:         ? Number(p.pos)
   93:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
   94:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
   95:           : null;
   96: 
   97:   return pos;
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
  101:   const directCandidates = [
  102:     p?.grupo2,
  103:     p?.group2,
  104:     p?.grupo,
  105:     p?.group,
  106:     p?.animal_grupo,
  107:     p?.grupo_animal,
  108:     p?.grupoAnimal,
  109:     p?.g,
  110:   ];
  111: 
  112:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
  113:     const g = Number(v);
  114:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
  115:       return g;
  116:     }
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
  120: 
  121:   if (milhar4) {
  122:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
  123:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
  124: 
  125:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   return null;
  129: }

  131: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {
  132:   return normalizeHourLike(
  133:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
  134:   );
  135: }

  137: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
  138:   const y =
  139:     draw?.ymd ||
  140:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
  141:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
  142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
  143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
  144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
  145:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
  146:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
  147:     null;
  148: 
  149:   return y;
  150: }

  152: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
  153:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  154:   if (prizes.length) {
  155:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
  156:     if (p1) {
  157:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
  158:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
  159:     }
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   const candidates = [
  163:     draw?.grupo1,
  164:     draw?.group1,
  165:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
  166:     draw?.first_grupo,
  167:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
  168:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
  169:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
  170:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
  171:     draw?.g1,
  172:     draw?.grupo_1,
  173:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
  174:   ];
  175: 
  176:   for (const v of candidates) {
  177:     if (Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return Number(v);
  178:   }
  179: 
  180:   return null;
  181: }

  183: /* =========================
  184:    Helpers internos

  186: 
  187: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
  188:   const n = Number(v);
  189:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
  190: }
  191: 
  192: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
  193:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
  194:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
  195: }
  196: 
  197: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
  198:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
  199:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
  200: 
  201:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  202: 
  203:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
  204:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
  205:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  206:   }
  207: 
  208:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
  209:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
  210: 
  211:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
  212: }

  214: /* =========================
  215:    Schedules
  216: ========================= */
  217: 
  218: const FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD = "2026-07-19";
  219: const PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD = "2026-07-18";
  220: const FEDERAL_20_REMOVES_PT_RIO_18_START_YMD = "2025-11-03";
  221: 
  222: /**
  223:  * Calendário Federal com preservação histórica.
  224:  *
  225:  * Até 18/07/2026:
  226:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
  227:  * - sábado às 20h.
  228:  *
  229:  * A partir de 19/07/2026:
  230:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
  231:  * - domingo às 11h;
  232:  * - sábado deixa de ter sorteio Federal.
  233:  */
  234: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
  235:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
  236:   if (!y) return false;
  237: 
  238:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
  239: 
  240:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD) {
  241:     return dow === 0 || dow === 3;
  242:   }
  243: 
  244:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
  245: }
  246: 
  247: function getFederalScheduleForYmd(ymd, FEDERAL_SCHEDULE) {
  248:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
  249: 
  250:   if (!y || !isFederalDrawDay(y)) {
  251:     return [];
  252:   }
  253: 
  254:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
  255: 
  256:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD && dow === 0) {
  257:     return ["11:00"];
  258:   }
  259: 
  260:   const historical = Array.isArray(FEDERAL_SCHEDULE)
  261:     ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE
  262:     : [];
  263: 
  264:   const normalized = historical
  265:     .map(toHourBucket)
  266:     .filter(Boolean);
  267: 
  268:   if (normalized.includes("20:00")) {
  269:     return ["20:00"];
  270:   }
  271: 
  272:   return normalized.length
  273:     ? normalized
  274:     : ["20:00"];
  275: }
  276: 
  277: /**
  278:  * Determina apenas os slots esperados.
  279:  * Nenhum resultado histórico é removido.
  280:  */
  281: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
  282:   ymd,
  283:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  284:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
  285: ) {
  286:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
  287:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
  288: 
  289:   if (dow === 0) {
  290:     const sundaySchedule = Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY)
  291:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY]
  292:       : [];
  293: 
  294:     if (y >= PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD) {
  295:       return sundaySchedule.filter((hour) => {
  296:         const h = normalizeHourLike(hour);
  297: 
  298:         return h === "14:00" || h === "16:00";
  299:       });
  300:     }
  301: 
  302:     return sundaySchedule;
  303:   }
  304: 
  305:   if (dow === 6) {
  306:     const saturdaySchedule = Array.isArray(
  307:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
  308:     )
  309:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
  310:       : [];
  311: 
  312:     if (y >= "2026-07-18") {
  313:       const transitionedSchedule = saturdaySchedule
  314:         .map(normalizeHourLike)
  315:         .filter(Boolean)
  316:         .filter((hour) => hour !== "18:00");
  317: 
  318:       if (!transitionedSchedule.includes("19:00")) {
  319:         transitionedSchedule.push("19:00");
  320:       }
  321: 
  322:       return transitionedSchedule
  323:         .filter(
  324:           (hour, index, schedule) =>
  325:             schedule.indexOf(hour) === index
  326:         )
  327:         .sort(
  328:           (hourA, hourB) =>
  329:             hourToInt(hourA) - hourToInt(hourB)
  330:         );
  331:     }
  332: 
  333:     return saturdaySchedule;
  334:   }
  335: 
  336:   if (dow === 3) {
  337:     return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT)
  338:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
  339:       : [];
  340:   }
  341: 
  342:   return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL)
  343:     ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL]
  344:     : [];
  345: }
  346: 
  347: export function getScheduleForLottery({
  348:   lotteryKey,
  349:   ymd,
  350:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  351:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  352:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  353: }) {
  354:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  355: 
  356:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  357:     return getFederalScheduleForYmd(
  358:       ymd,
  359:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE
  360:     );
  361:   }
  362: 
  363:   if (key === "LOOK") {
  364:     return [...LOOK_SCHEDULE];
  365:   }
  366: 
  367:   if (key === "NACIONAL") {
  368:     return [...NACIONAL_SCHEDULE];
  369:   }
  370: 
  371:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
  372:     ymd,
  373:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  374:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
  375:   );
  376: }

  378: export function scheduleSet(schedule) {
  379:   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));
  380: }
  381: 
  382: export function isHourInSchedule(schedule, hhmm) {
  383:   const s = scheduleSet(schedule);
  384:   return s.has(toHourBucket(hhmm));
  385: }
  386: 
  387: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
  388:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  389:   if (!list.length) return null;
  390: 
  391:   const sorted = [...list]
  392:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  393:     .sort((a, b) => {
  394:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  395:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  396:       return tb - ta;
  397:     });
  398: 
  399:   return sorted[0] || null;
  400: }
  401: 
  402: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
  403:   draws,
  404:   targetHourBucket,
  405:   schedule
  406: ) {
  407:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  408:   if (!list.length) return null;
  409: 
  410:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
  411:   if (t < 0) return null;
  412: 
  413:   const candidates = list
  414:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
  415:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
  416:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
  417:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
  418: 
  419:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
  420: }
  421: 
  422: export async function getPreviousDrawRobust({
  423:   getKingResultsByDate,
  424:   lotteryKey,
  425:   ymdTarget,
  426:   targetHourBucket,
  427:   todayDraws,
  428:   schedule,
  429:   maxBackDays = 10,
  430:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  431:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  432:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  433: }) {
  434:   const prevSameDay = findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
  435:     todayDraws,
  436:     targetHourBucket,
  437:     schedule
  438:   );
  439: 
  440:   if (prevSameDay) {
  441:     return {
  442:       draw: prevSameDay,
  443:       ymd: pickDrawYMD(prevSameDay) || ymdTarget,
  444:       hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevSameDay)),
  445:       source: "same_day",
  446:     };
  447:   }
  448: 
  449:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxBackDays; i += 1) {
  450:     const day = addDaysYMD(ymdTarget, -i);
  451: 
  452:     const daySchedule = getScheduleForLottery({
  453:       lotteryKey,
  454:       ymd: day,
  455:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  456:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  457:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  458:     });
  459: 
  460:     if (
  461:       safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase() === "FEDERAL" &&
  462:       !daySchedule.length
  463:     ) {
  464:       continue;
  465:     }
  466: 
  467:     const out = await getKingResultsByDate({
  468:       uf: lotteryKey,
  469:       date: day,
  470:       closeHour: null,
  471:       positions: null,
  472:       readPolicy: "server",
  473:     });
  474: 
  475:     const last = findLastDrawInList(out, daySchedule);
  476:     if (last) {
  477:       return {
  478:         draw: last,
  479:         ymd: pickDrawYMD(last) || day,
  480:         hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(last)),
  481:         source: "prev_day",
  482:       };
  483:     }
  484:   }
  485: 
  486:   return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
  487: }

  489: /* =========================

  492: 
  493: export function getNextSlotForLottery({
  494:   lotteryKey,
  495:   ymd,
  496:   hourBucket,
  497:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  498:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  499:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  500:   maxForwardDays = 21,
  501: }) {
  502:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  503:   const y0 = safeStr(ymd);
  504:   const h0 = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
  505: 
  506:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !safeStr(h0)) return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
  507: 
  508:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  509:     for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
  510:       const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
  511:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
  512:         lotteryKey: key,
  513:         ymd: day,
  514:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  515:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  516:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  517:       });
  518:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
  519:         return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
  520:       }
  521:     }
  522:     return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
  523:   }
  524: 
  525:   const sch0 = getScheduleForLottery({
  526:     lotteryKey: key,
  527:     ymd: y0,
  528:     hourBucket: h0,
  529:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  530:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  531:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  532:   });
  533: 
  534:   const idx = (Array.isArray(sch0) ? sch0 : []).findIndex(
  535:     (x) => toHourBucket(x) === h0
  536:   );
  537: 
  538:   if (idx >= 0 && idx < sch0.length - 1) {
  539:     return { ymd: y0, hour: toHourBucket(sch0[idx + 1]) };
  540:   }
  541: 
  542:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
  543:     const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
  544:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
  545:       lotteryKey: key,
  546:       ymd: day,
  547:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  548:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  549:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  550:     });
  551:     if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
  552:       return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
  553:     }
  554:   }
  555: 
  556:   return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
  557: }

  559: /* =========================
  560:    Próximo DRAW REAL (não perde amostra)
  561: ========================= */
  562: 
  563: export function findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
  564:   lotteryKey,
  565:   startSlot,
  566:   drawsIndex,
  567:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  568:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  569:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  570:   maxSteps = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_STEPS,
  571:   maxDays = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_DAYS,
  572: }) {
  573:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  574:   const y0 = safeStr(startSlot?.ymd);
  575:   const h0 = toHourBucket(startSlot?.hour);
  576: 
  577:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !h0 || !(drawsIndex instanceof Map)) {
  578:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
  579:   }
  580: 
  581:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  582:     let curY = y0;
  583:     let steps = 0;
  584:     let daysWalked = 0;
  585: 
  586:     while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
  587:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
  588:         lotteryKey: key,
  589:         ymd: curY,
  590:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  591:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  592:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  593:       });
  594: 
  595:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
  596:         const hh = toHourBucket(sch[0]);
  597:         const d = drawsIndex.get(`${curY}|${hh}`) || null;
  598:         if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: hh }, draw: d };
  599:       }
  600: 
  601:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
  602:       daysWalked += 1;
  603:       steps += 1;
  604:     }
  605: 
  606:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
  607:   }
  608: 
  609:   let curY = y0;
  610:   let curH = h0;
  611:   let steps = 0;
  612:   let daysWalked = 0;
  613: 
  614:   while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
  615:     const drawKey = `${curY}|${toHourBucket(curH)}`;
  616:     let d = drawsIndex.get(drawKey) || null;
  617: 
  618:     if (!d) {
  619:       const curTs = ymdHourToTs(curY, curH);
  620:       const curYLocked = curY;
  621:       d = Array.from(drawsIndex.entries())
  622:         .map(([k, v]) => {
  623:           const [ky, kh] = String(k).split("|");
  624:           return { ymd: ky, hour: kh, draw: v, ts: ymdHourToTs(ky, kh) };
  625:         })
  626:         .filter((x) => x.ymd === curYLocked)
  627:         .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts >= curTs)
  628:         .sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
  629:     }
  630: 
  631:     if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: toHourBucket(curH) }, draw: d };
  632: 
  633:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
  634:       lotteryKey: key,
  635:       ymd: curY,
  636:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  637:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  638:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  639:     });
  640: 
  641:     const arr = Array.isArray(sch) ? sch.map(toHourBucket) : [];
  642:     const curBucket = toHourBucket(curH);
  643:     const idx = arr.indexOf(curBucket);
  644: 
  645:     if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length - 1) {
  646:       curH = arr[idx + 1];
  647:     } else {
  648:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
  649:       daysWalked += 1;
  650: 
  651:       const sch2 = getScheduleForLottery({
  652:         lotteryKey: key,
  653:         ymd: curY,
  654:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  655:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  656:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  657:       });
  658: 
  659:       const arr2 = Array.isArray(sch2) ? sch2.map(toHourBucket) : [];
  660:       if (!arr2.length) break;
  661:       curH = arr2[0];
  662:     }
  663: 
  664:     steps += 1;
  665:   }
  666: 
  667:   return { slot: null, draw: null };
  668: }
  669: 
  670: /* =========================
  671:    Index + utilitários de grupo
  672: ========================= */
  673: 
  674: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
  675:   const map = new Map();
  676:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  677: 
  678:   for (const d of list) {
  679:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  680:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
  681:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
  682: 
  683:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  684: 
  685:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
  686:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
  687: 
  688:     if (!prev) {
  689:       map.set(drawKey, d);
  690:       continue;
  691:     }
  692: 
  693:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
  694:       map.set(drawKey, d);
  695:     }
  696:   }
  697: 
  698:   return map;
  699: }
  700: 
  701: export function computeLastSeenByGrupo(draws) {
  702:   const last = new Map();
  703:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  704: 
  705:   for (const d of list) {
  706:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  707:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  708:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  709: 
  710:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  711:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
  712: 
  713:     for (const p of ps) {
  714:       const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
  715:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
  716:         continue;
  717:       }
  718: 
  719:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
  720:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
  721: 
  722:       const gg = Number(g);
  723:       const prev = last.get(gg);
  724: 
  725:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
  726:         last.set(gg, ts);
  727:       }
  728:     }
  729:   }
  730: 
  731:   return last;
  732: }
  733: 
  734: export function countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(draw) {
  735:   const counts = new Map();
  736:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  737:   for (const p of ps) {
  738:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
  739:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
  740:       continue;
  741:     }
  742:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
  743:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
  744:     const gg = Number(g);
  745:     counts.set(gg, (counts.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  746:   }
  747:   return counts;
  748: }
  749: 
  750: function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
  751:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  752:   const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
  753:   if (p1) {
  754:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
  755:     return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
  756:   }
  757:   return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
  758: }
  759: 
  760: function getPrizeGroupsByPosition(draw, maxPosition = 3) {
  761:   const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxPosition || 3));
  762:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  763: 
  764:   return Array.from({ length: limit }, (_, index) => {
  765:     const position = index + 1;
  766: 
  767:     const prize =
  768:       prizes.find(
  769:         (item) => Number(guessPrizePos(item)) === position
  770:       ) || null;
  771: 
  772:     if (!prize) return null;
  773: 
  774:     const grupo = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  775: 
  776:     return Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
  777:       grupo >= 1 &&
  778:       grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  779:       ? grupo
  780:       : null;
  781:   });
  782: }
  783: 
  784: 
  785: function getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw) {
  786:   const out = [];
  787:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  788:   for (const p of ps) {
  789:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
  790:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
  791:       continue;
  792:     }
  793:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
  794:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
  795:     out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
  796:   }
  797:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
  798: }
  799: 
  800: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
  801:   const freq = new Map();
  802:   const firstMap = new Map();
  803:   const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(nextDraw);
  804: 
  805:   for (const it of items) {
  806:     freq.set(it.grupo, (freq.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
  807:     if (it.pos === 1) {
  808:       firstMap.set(it.grupo, (firstMap.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
  809:     }
  810:   }
  811: 
  812:   return { freq, firstMap };
  813: }
  814: 
  815: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
  816:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
  817:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
  818:   }
  819: }
  820: 
  821: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  822:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  823:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
  824:   const out = new Map();
  825: 
  826:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  827:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  828:     out.set(g, n / denom);
  829:   }
  830: 
  831:   return out;
  832: }
  833: 
  834: function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  835:   draws,
  836:   lotteryKey,
  837:   targetHour,
  838:   targetDow,
  839:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  840: ) {
  841:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  842:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  843:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  844:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  845: 
  846:   const freq = new Map();
  847:   let totalSamples = 0;
  848: 
  849:   for (const d of list) {
  850:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  851:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  852:     if (!h || !y) continue;
  853: 
  854:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  855:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  856:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  857:     } else {
  858:       if (h !== target) continue;
  859:     }
  860: 
  861:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  862:     if (!items.length) continue;
  863: 
  864:     totalSamples += 1;
  865: 
  866:     for (const it of items) {
  867:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  868:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  869:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  870:     }
  871:   }
  872: 
  873:   const out = new Map();
  874:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  875: 
  876:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  877:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
  878:     out.set(g, n / denom);
  879:   }
  880: 
  881:   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
  882: }
  883: 
  884: /* =========================
  885:    Recência / pressão estrutural
  886: ========================= */
  887: 
  888: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
  889:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  890:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
  891:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
  892:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
  893:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
  894:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
  895:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
  896:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
  897:     return tsa - tsb;
  898:   });
  899: }
  900: 
  901: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
  902:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  903:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  904:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  905:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  906: 
  907:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
  908:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  909:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  910:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  911:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
  912:   });
  913: 
  914:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  915: }
  916: 
  917: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  918:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  919:   const out = new Map();
  920: 
  921:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  922:     out.set(g, {
  923:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
  924:       recentIndirect: 0,
  925:       recentFirst: 0,
  926:       recentLast1First: 0,
  927:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
  928:     });
  929:   }
  930: 
  931:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
  932:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
  933:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
  934: 
  935:   for (const d of list) {
  936:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  937: 
  938:     for (const it of items) {
  939:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  940:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
  941:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  942: 
  943:       const row = out.get(g);
  944:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
  945: 
  946:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
  947:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
  948:     }
  949:   }
  950: 
  951:   for (const d of last1) {
  952:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  953:     for (const it of items) {
  954:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  955:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
  956:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  957:       if (pos === 1) {
  958:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
  959:       }
  960:     }
  961:   }
  962: 
  963:   for (const d of last2) {
  964:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  965:     const seen = new Set();
  966:     for (const it of items) {
  967:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  968:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  969:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
  970:       seen.add(g);
  971:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
  972:     }
  973:   }
  974: 
  975:   return out;
  976: }
  977: 
  978: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
  979:   const v = Number(value || 0);
  980:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
  981:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
  982:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
  983: }
  984: 
  985: function clamp01(v) {
  986:   const n = Number(v);
  987:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
  988:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
  989: }
  990: 
  991: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  992:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  993:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
  994: }
  995: 
  996: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
  997:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
  998:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
  999:   return clamp01(n / lim);
 1000: }

 1006: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
 1007:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
 1008:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
 1009:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
 1010:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
 1011:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
 1012:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
 1013: 
 1014:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
 1015:   FED_PREVG: 4,
 1016:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
 1017:   FED_ONLY: 10,
 1018: };

 1020: function buildLayerConfigs({
 1021:   lotteryKey,
 1022:   targetDayOfMonth,
 1023:   targetDow,
 1024:   prevHour,
 1025:   prevGrupo,
 1026:   targetHour,
 1027: }) {
 1028:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 1029: 
 1030:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
 1031:     return [
 1032:       {
 1033:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
 1034:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
 1035:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
 1036:         match: (ctx) =>
 1037:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
 1038:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
 1039:       },
 1040:       {
 1041:         key: "FED_PREVG",
 1042:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
 1043:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
 1044:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
 1045:       },
 1046:       {
 1047:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
 1048:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
 1049:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
 1050:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
 1051:       },
 1052:       {
 1053:         key: "FED_ONLY",
 1054:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
 1055:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
 1056:         match: () => true,
 1057:       },
 1058:     ];
 1059:   }
 1060: 
 1061:   return [
 1062:     {
 1063:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
 1064:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
 1065:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
 1066:       match: (ctx) =>
 1067:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
 1068:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
 1069:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
 1070:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
 1071:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1072:     },
 1073:     {
 1074:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
 1075:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
 1076:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
 1077:       match: (ctx) =>
 1078:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
 1079:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
 1080:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
 1081:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1082:     },
 1083:     {
 1084:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
 1085:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
 1086:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
 1087:       match: (ctx) =>
 1088:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
 1089:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
 1090:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1091:     },
 1092:     {
 1093:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
 1094:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
 1095:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
 1096:       match: (ctx) =>
 1097:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
 1098:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1099:     },
 1100:     {
 1101:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
 1102:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
 1103:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
 1104:       match: (ctx) =>
 1105:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
 1106:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1107:     },
 1108:     {
 1109:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
 1110:       label: "alvo geral",
 1111:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
 1112:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
 1113:     },
 1114:   ];
 1115: }
 1116: 
 1117: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
 1118:   lotteryKey,
 1119:   drawsRange,
 1120:   targetHour,
 1121:   targetDow,
 1122:   targetDayOfMonth,
 1123:   prevHour,
 1124:   prevGrupo,
 1125:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1126:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1127:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1128: }) {
 1129:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
 1130:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
 1131: 
 1132:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
 1133:     lotteryKey,
 1134:     targetDayOfMonth,
 1135:     targetDow,
 1136:     prevHour,
 1137:     prevGrupo,
 1138:     targetHour,
 1139:   });
 1140: 
 1141:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
 1142:     key: layer.key,
 1143:     label: layer.label,
 1144:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 1145:     samples: 0,
 1146:     freq: new Map(),
 1147:     firstFreq: new Map(),
 1148:     matchingDraws: [],
 1149:   }));
 1150: 
 1151:   for (const d of list) {
 1152:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
 1153:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 1154:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
 1155: 
 1156:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 1157:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
 1158: 
 1159:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
 1160:       lotteryKey,
 1161:       ymd: prevY,
 1162:       hourBucket: prevH,
 1163:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1164:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1165:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1166:     });
 1167: 
 1168:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
 1169: 
 1170:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
 1171:       lotteryKey,
 1172:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
 1173:       drawsIndex,
 1174:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1175:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1176:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1177:     });
 1178: 
 1179:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
 1180:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
 1181:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
 1182: 
 1183:     const ctx = {
 1184:       prevYmd: prevY,
 1185:       prevHour: prevH,
 1186:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
 1187:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
 1188:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
 1189:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
 1190:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
 1191:     };
 1192: 
 1193:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
 1194: 
 1195:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
 1196:       const layer = layers[i];
 1197:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
 1198: 
 1199:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
 1200:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
 1201:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
 1202:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
 1203:     }
 1204:   }
 1205: 
 1206:   let chosen =
 1207:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
 1208:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
 1209:     null;
 1210: 
 1211:   if (!chosen) {
 1212:     chosen = {
 1213:       key: "NONE",
 1214:       label: "sem amostra",
 1215:       minSamples: 0,
 1216:       samples: 0,
 1217:       freq: new Map(),
 1218:       firstFreq: new Map(),
 1219:       matchingDraws: [],
 1220:     };
 1221:   }
 1222: 
 1223:   return {
 1224:     chosen,
 1225:     layers: layerResults,
 1226:   };
 1227: }
 1228: 
 1229: /* =========================
 1230:    Motor principal V1
 1231: ========================= */
 1232: 
 1233: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
 1234:   lotteryKey,
 1235:   drawsRange,
 1236:   drawLast,
 1237:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1238:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1239:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1240:   topN = 3,
 1241:   targetYmdOverride = "",
 1242:   targetHourOverride = "",
 1243: }) {
 1244:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
 1245:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
 1246:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 1247:   }
 1248: 
 1249:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 1250: 
 1251:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
 1252:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
 1253:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
 1254: 
 1255:   if (
 1256:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
 1257:     !lastH ||
 1258:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
 1259:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
 1260:   ) {
 1261:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 1262:   }
 1263: 
 1264:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
 1265:     lotteryKey,
 1266:     ymd: lastY,
 1267:     hourBucket: lastH,
 1268:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1269:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1270:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1271:   });
 1272: 
 1273:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
 1274:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
 1275: 
 1276:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 1277:     ? forcedTargetY
 1278:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
 1279: 
 1280:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 1281:     ? forcedTargetH
 1282:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
 1283:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
 1284:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
 1285:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
 1286: 
 1287:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
 1288:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
 1289:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
 1290: 
 1291:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
 1292:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 1293:   }
 1294: 
 1295:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
 1296:     lotteryKey,
 1297:     drawsRange: list,
 1298:     targetHour: targetH,
 1299:     targetDow,
 1300:     targetDayOfMonth,
 1301:     prevHour: lastH,
 1302:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 1303:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 1304:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 1305:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 1306:   });
 1307: 
 1308:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
 1309:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
 1310:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
 1311:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
 1312:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
 1313: 
 1314:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
 1315:     list,
 1316:     key,
 1317:     targetH,
 1318:     targetDow,
 1319:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 1320:   );
 1321:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
 1322: 
 1323:   const condWeight =
 1324:     key === "FEDERAL"
 1325:       ? samples >= 8
 1326:         ? 0.55
 1327:         : samples >= 4
 1328:           ? 0.4
 1329:           : 0.25
 1330:       : samples >= 12
 1331:         ? 0.65
 1332:         : samples >= 6
 1333:           ? 0.55
 1334:           : 0.45;
 1335: 
 1336:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
 1337: 
 1338:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
 1339:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
 1340: 
 1341:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
 1342:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
 1343:   );
 1344:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
 1345:   const maxGapMsBase =
 1346:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
 1347:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
 1348:       : 1;
 1349: 
 1350:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
 1351:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
 1352: 
 1353:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
 1354:     1,
 1355:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
 1356:   );
 1357:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
 1358:     1,
 1359:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
 1360:   );
 1361:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
 1362:     1,
 1363:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
 1364:   );
 1365: 
 1366:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
 1367:     const grupo = idx + 1;
 1368:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
 1369:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
 1370:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
 1371:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
 1372:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
 1373: 
 1374:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
 1375:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
 1376: 
 1377:     const gapMs =
 1378:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
 1379:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
 1380:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
 1381:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
 1382:         : maxGapMsBase;
 1383: 
 1384:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
 1385:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
 1386:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
 1387: 
 1388:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
 1389:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
 1390:       recentIndirect: 0,
 1391:       recentFirst: 0,
 1392:       recentLast1First: 0,
 1393:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
 1394:     };
 1395: 
 1396:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
 1397:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
 1398:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
 1399:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
 1400:     const recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
 1401: 
 1402:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
 1403:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
 1404:         ? 0.72
 1405:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
 1406:           ? 0.84
 1407:           : 1.0;
 1408: 
 1409:     const smartLateBoost =
 1410:       lateBonus *
 1411:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm * 0.2)) *
 1412:       recencyCoolingFactor;
 1413: 
 1414:     const recentComposite =
 1415:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.45) +
 1416:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
 1417:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
 1418: 
 1419:     const baseScore =
 1420:       key === "FEDERAL"
 1421:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
 1422:           (pBase * 85) +
 1423:           (primeiros * 42) +
 1424:           (aparicoes * 18) +
 1425:           (recentComposite * 26) +
 1426:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
 1427:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
 1428:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
 1429:             (aparicoes * 155) +
 1430:             (primeiros * 70) +
 1431:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
 1432:             (recentComposite * 18) +
 1433:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
 1434:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
 1435:           : (pFinal * 125) +
 1436:             (aparicoes * 135) +
 1437:             (primeiros * 62) +
 1438:             (recentComposite * 24) +
 1439:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
 1440:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
 1441: 
 1442:     return {
 1443:       grupo,
 1444:       aparicoes,
 1445:       primeiros,
 1446:       taxaPrimeiro,
 1447:       scoreProb: pFinal,
 1448:       probCond: pCond,
 1449:       probBase: pBase,
 1450:       score: baseScore,
 1451:       lateBonus,
 1452:       smartLateBoost,
 1453:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
 1454:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
 1455:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
 1456:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
 1457:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
 1458:       recentComposite,
 1459:       lastSeenTs,
 1460:       gapMs,
 1461:     };
 1462:   })
 1463:     .sort((a, b) => {
 1464:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
 1465:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
 1466:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
 1467:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
 1468:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
 1469:     })
 1470:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
 1471: 
 1472:   const reasonsBase = [
 1473:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
 1474:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
 1475:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
 1476:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
 1477:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
 1478:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
 1479:   ];
 1480: 
 1481:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
 1482:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
 1483:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
 1484:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
 1485:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
 1486: 
 1487:     return {
 1488:       rank: idx + 1,
 1489:       title:
 1490:         idx === 0
 1491:           ? "Mais provável"
 1492:           : idx === 1
 1493:             ? "2º mais provável"
 1494:             : "3º mais provável",
 1495:       grupo: x.grupo,
 1496:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
 1497:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
 1498:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
 1499:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
 1500:       freq: x.aparicoes,
 1501:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
 1502:       freqBase: 0,
 1503:       freqZeroWhy:
 1504:         x.aparicoes <= 0
 1505:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
 1506:           : "",
 1507:       reasons: [
 1508:         ...reasonsBase,
 1509:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
 1510:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
 1511:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
 1512:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
 1513:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
 1514:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentPrizePresence}`,
 1515:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 1516:       ],
 1517:       meta: {
 1518:         trigger: {
 1519:           ymd: lastY,
 1520:           hour: lastH,
 1521:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 1522:         },
 1523:         next: {
 1524:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 1525:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
 1526:         },
 1527:         samples,
 1528:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 1529:         explain: {
 1530:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 1531:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
 1532:           layerSamples: samples,
 1533:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
 1534:           condWeight,
 1535:           baseWeight,
 1536:           targetDow,
 1537:           targetDayOfMonth,
 1538:           prevHour: lastH,
 1539:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 1540:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
 1541:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
 1542:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
 1543:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
 1544:           recentLast2PrizePresence: x.recentLast2PrizePresence,
 1545:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
 1546:             key: layer.key,
 1547:             label: layer.label,
 1548:             samples: layer.samples,
 1549:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 1550:           })),
 1551:         },
 1552:       },
 1553:     };
 1554:   });
 1555: 
 1556:   return {
 1557:     top,
 1558:     meta: {
 1559:       trigger: {
 1560:         ymd: lastY,
 1561:         hour: lastH,
 1562:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 1563:       },
 1564:       next: {
 1565:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 1566:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
 1567:       },
 1568:       samples,
 1569:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 1570:       explain: {
 1571:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
 1572:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
 1573:         layerSamples: samples,
 1574:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
 1575:         condWeight,
 1576:         baseWeight,
 1577:         targetDow,
 1578:         targetDayOfMonth,
 1579:         prevHour: lastH,
 1580:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 1581:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
 1582:           key: layer.key,
 1583:           label: layer.label,
 1584:           samples: layer.samples,
 1585:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 1586:         })),
 1587:       },
 1588:     },
 1589:   };
 1590: }
 1591: 
 1592: /* =========================
 1593:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
 1594: ========================= */
 1595: 
 1596: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
 1597:   draws,
 1598:   lotteryKey,
 1599:   targetHour,
 1600:   targetDow,
 1601:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 1602: ) {
 1603:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 1604:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 1605:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
 1606:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 1607: 
 1608:   const firstFreq = new Map();
 1609:   let totalSamples = 0;
 1610: 
 1611:   for (const d of list) {
 1612:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 1613:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 1614:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
 1615: 
 1616:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
 1617:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
 1618:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
 1619:     } else {
 1620:       if (h !== target) continue;
 1621:     }
 1622: 
 1623:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 1624:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
 1625: 
 1626:     totalSamples += 1;
 1627:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
 1628:   }
 1629: 
 1630:   const prob = new Map();
 1631:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
 1632: 
 1633:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1634:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 1635:   }
 1636: 
 1637:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
 1638: }
 1639: 
 1640: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
 1641:   draws,
 1642:   lotteryKey,
 1643:   targetHour,
 1644:   targetDow
 1645: ) {
 1646:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
 1647:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 1648:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
 1649: 
 1650:   return list.filter((d) => {
 1651:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 1652:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 1653:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
 1654: 
 1655:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
 1656:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
 1657:     }
 1658: 
 1659:     return h === target;
 1660:   });
 1661: }
 1662: 
 1663: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
 1664:   draws,
 1665:   lotteryKey,
 1666:   targetHour,
 1667:   targetDow
 1668: ) {
 1669:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
 1670:     draws,
 1671:     lotteryKey,
 1672:     targetHour,
 1673:     targetDow
 1674:   )
 1675:     .map((d) => {
 1676:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 1677:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 1678:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 1679:       return {
 1680:         draw: d,
 1681:         ymd: y,
 1682:         hour: h,
 1683:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
 1684:         grupo: Number(grupo),
 1685:       };
 1686:     })
 1687:     .filter((x) =>
 1688:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
 1689:       x.hour &&
 1690:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
 1691:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
 1692:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
 1693:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
 1694:     );
 1695: }
 1696: 
 1697: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
 1698:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
 1699:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
 1700: 
 1701:   return list
 1702:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
 1703:     .slice(-n)
 1704:     .reverse()
 1705:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 1706:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
 1707: }
 1708: 
 1709: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
 1710:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
 1711:   return list
 1712:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
 1713:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
 1714: }
 1715: 
 1716: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
 1717:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
 1718:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
 1719:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
 1720: 
 1721:   let matched = 0;
 1722:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
 1723:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
 1724:     matched += 1;
 1725:   }
 1726:   return matched;
 1727: }
 1728: 
 1729: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
 1730:   drawsRange,
 1731:   lotteryKey,
 1732:   targetHour,
 1733:   targetDow,
 1734:   currentState,
 1735:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
 1736: }) {
 1737:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
 1738:     drawsRange,
 1739:     lotteryKey,
 1740:     targetHour,
 1741:     targetDow
 1742:   );
 1743: 
 1744:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 1745:   const freq = new Map();
 1746: 
 1747:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1748:     freq.set(g, 0);
 1749:   }
 1750: 
 1751:   let totalWeight = 0;
 1752:   let matchedSamples = 0;
 1753: 
 1754:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
 1755:     const target = seq[i];
 1756:     const histState = [];
 1757: 
 1758:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
 1759:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
 1760:     }
 1761: 
 1762:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
 1763:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
 1764: 
 1765:     const weight =
 1766:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
 1767:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
 1768:       1.0;
 1769: 
 1770:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
 1771:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
 1772: 
 1773:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
 1774:     totalWeight += weight;
 1775:     matchedSamples += 1;
 1776:   }
 1777: 
 1778:   const prob = new Map();
 1779:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
 1780: 
 1781:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1782:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 1783:   }
 1784: 
 1785:   return {
 1786:     prob,
 1787:     freq,
 1788:     totalWeight,
 1789:     matchedSamples,
 1790:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
 1791:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
 1792:   };
 1793: }
 1794: 
 1795: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
 1796:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
 1797:   const groups = list
 1798:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
 1799:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
 1800: 
 1801:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
 1802:     return {
 1803:       regime: "neutral",
 1804:       repeatRate: 0,
 1805:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
 1806:       samples: groups.length,
 1807:     };
 1808:   }
 1809: 
 1810:   let repeats = 0;
 1811:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
 1812:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
 1813:   }
 1814: 
 1815:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
 1816:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
 1817: 
 1818:   let regime = "neutral";
 1819: 
 1820:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
 1821:     regime = "repeat";
 1822:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
 1823:     regime = "spread";
 1824:   }
 1825: 
 1826:   return {
 1827:     regime,
 1828:     repeatRate,
 1829:     uniqueRate,
 1830:     samples: groups.length,
 1831:   };
 1832: }

 1834: function computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
 1835:   draws,
 1836:   lotteryKey,
 1837:   targetHour,
 1838:   targetDow,
 1839:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 1840: ) {
 1841:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
 1842:     draws,
 1843:     lotteryKey,
 1844:     targetHour,
 1845:     targetDow
 1846:   );
 1847: 
 1848:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 1849:   const freq = new Map();
 1850:   let totalSamples = 0;
 1851: 
 1852:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1853:     freq.set(g, 0);
 1854:   }
 1855: 
 1856:   for (const d of list) {
 1857:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
 1858:     if (!items.length) continue;
 1859: 
 1860:     totalSamples += 1;
 1861: 
 1862:     for (const it of items) {
 1863:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 1864:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
 1865:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
 1866:     }
 1867:   }
 1868: 
 1869:   const prob = new Map();
 1870:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
 1871: 
 1872:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1873:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 1874:   }
 1875: 
 1876:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
 1877: }

 1879: function computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
 1880:   draws,
 1881:   lotteryKey,
 1882:   targetHour,
 1883:   targetDow,
 1884:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 1885: ) {
 1886:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
 1887:     draws,
 1888:     lotteryKey,
 1889:     targetHour,
 1890:     targetDow
 1891:   );
 1892: 
 1893:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 1894:   const freq = new Map();
 1895:   let totalSamples = 0;
 1896: 
 1897:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1898:     freq.set(g, 0);
 1899:   }
 1900: 
 1901:   for (const d of list) {
 1902:     const counts = countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(d);
 1903:     if (!counts.size) continue;
 1904: 
 1905:     totalSamples += 1;
 1906: 
 1907:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1908:       const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
 1909:       if (c >= 2) {
 1910:         freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
 1911:       }
 1912:     }
 1913:   }
 1914: 
 1915:   const prob = new Map();
 1916:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
 1917: 
 1918:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1919:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 1920:   }
 1921: 
 1922:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
 1923: }

 1925: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
 1926:   draws,
 1927:   lotteryKey,
 1928:   targetHour,
 1929:   targetDow,
 1930:   beforeTs,
 1931:   count = 8
 1932: ) {
 1933:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
 1934:     .filter((d) => {
 1935:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 1936:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 1937:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 1938:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
 1939:     })
 1940:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
 1941: }

 1943: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 1944:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 1945:   const out = new Map();
 1946: 
 1947:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 1948:     out.set(g, {
 1949:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
 1950:       recentFirst: 0,
 1951:       recentDupDraws: 0,
 1952:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
 1953:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
 1954:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
 1955:     });
 1956:   }
 1957: 
 1958:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 1959:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
 1960:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
 1961:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
 1962: 
 1963:   for (const d of list) {
 1964:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
 1965:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
 1966: 
 1967:     for (const it of items) {
 1968:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 1969:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
 1970: 
 1971:       const row = out.get(g);
 1972:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
 1973: 
 1974:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
 1975:         row.recentFirst += 1;
 1976:       }
 1977: 
 1978:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
 1979:     }
 1980: 
 1981:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
 1982:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
 1983:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
 1984:       }
 1985:     }
 1986:   }
 1987: 
 1988:   for (const d of last1) {
 1989:     const seen = new Set();
 1990:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
 1991:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 1992:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
 1993:       seen.add(g);
 1994:       out.get(g).recentLast1PrizePresence += 1;
 1995:     }
 1996:   }
 1997: 
 1998:   for (const d of last2) {
 1999:     const seen = new Set();
 2000:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
 2001:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 2002:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
 2003:       seen.add(g);
 2004:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
 2005:     }
 2006:   }
 2007: 
 2008:   for (const d of last3) {
 2009:     const seen = new Set();
 2010:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
 2011:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 2012:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
 2013:       seen.add(g);
 2014:       out.get(g).recentLast3PrizePresence += 1;
 2015:     }
 2016:   }
 2017: 
 2018:   return out;
 2019: }

 2021: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
 2022:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
 2023:     return {
 2024:       transition: 0.25,
 2025:       pair: 0.25,
 2026:       memory: 0.2,
 2027:       recent: 0.15,
 2028:       structural: 0.1,
 2029:       late: 0.05,
 2030:     };
 2031:   }
 2032: 
 2033:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
 2034:     return {
 2035:       transition: 0.2,
 2036:       pair: 0.1,
 2037:       memory: 0.1,
 2038:       recent: 0.2,
 2039:       structural: 0.3,
 2040:       late: 0.1,
 2041:     };
 2042:   }
 2043: 
 2044:   return {
 2045:     transition: 0.3,
 2046:     pair: 0.15,
 2047:     memory: 0.15,
 2048:     recent: 0.15,
 2049:     structural: 0.2,
 2050:     late: 0.05,
 2051:   };
 2052: }
 2053: 
 2054: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
 2055:   const safe = {
 2056:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
 2057:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
 2058:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
 2059:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
 2060:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
 2061:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
 2062:   };
 2063: 
 2064:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
 2065:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
 2066:     return {
 2067:       transition: 0.3,
 2068:       pair: 0.15,
 2069:       memory: 0.15,
 2070:       recent: 0.15,
 2071:       structural: 0.2,
 2072:       late: 0.05,
 2073:     };
 2074:   }
 2075: 
 2076:   return {
 2077:     transition: safe.transition / total,
 2078:     pair: safe.pair / total,
 2079:     memory: safe.memory / total,
 2080:     recent: safe.recent / total,
 2081:     structural: safe.structural / total,
 2082:     late: safe.late / total,
 2083:   };
 2084: }

 2086: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
 2087:   draws,
 2088:   lotteryKey,
 2089:   targetHour,
 2090:   targetDow,
 2091:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2092: ) {
 2093:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
 2094:     draws,
 2095:     lotteryKey,
 2096:     targetHour,
 2097:     targetDow
 2098:   );
 2099: 
 2100:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 2101:   const last = new Map();
 2102: 
 2103:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2104:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
 2105:   }
 2106: 
 2107:   for (const d of list) {
 2108:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 2109:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 2110:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 2111:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
 2112: 
 2113:     for (const it of items) {
 2114:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
 2115:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
 2116: 
 2117:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
 2118:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
 2119:         last.set(g, ts);
 2120:       }
 2121:     }
 2122:   }
 2123: 
 2124:   return last;
 2125: }
 2126: 
 2127: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
 2128:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
 2129:   const freq = new Map();
 2130:   const firstFreq = new Map();
 2131:   const seq = [];
 2132: 
 2133:   for (const d of list) {
 2134:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 2135:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
 2136: 
 2137:     const gg = Number(g);
 2138:     seq.push(gg);
 2139:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
 2140: 
 2141:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
 2142:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
 2143:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
 2144:     }
 2145:   }
 2146: 
 2147:   const total = seq.length;
 2148:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
 2149:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
 2150: 
 2151:   let dominante = null;
 2152:   let dominanteCount = -1;
 2153:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
 2154:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
 2155:       dominante = Number(g);
 2156:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
 2157:     }
 2158:   }
 2159: 
 2160:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
 2161:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
 2162:   const repeatNow =
 2163:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
 2164:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
 2165:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
 2166: 
 2167:   return {
 2168:     total,
 2169:     unique,
 2170:     diversidade,
 2171:     dominante,
 2172:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
 2173:     repeatNow,
 2174:     freq,
 2175:     firstFreq,
 2176:     seq,
 2177:   };
 2178: }
 2179: 
 2180: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
 2181:   drawsRange,
 2182:   lotteryKey,
 2183:   targetHour,
 2184:   targetDow,
 2185:   targetDayOfMonth,
 2186:   prevPair,
 2187:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2188:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2189:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2190:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
 2191: }) {
 2192:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
 2193:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
 2194:     lotteryKey,
 2195:     targetHour,
 2196:     targetDow
 2197:   );
 2198: 
 2199:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 2200:   const outFreq = new Map();
 2201: 
 2202:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
 2203: 
 2204:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
 2205:   if (pair.length < 2) {
 2206:     return {
 2207:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
 2208:       samples: 0,
 2209:       weightedSamples: 0,
 2210:       exactDomSamples: 0,
 2211:       freq: outFreq,
 2212:     };
 2213:   }
 2214: 
 2215:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
 2216:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
 2217:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 2218: 
 2219:   let matchedSamples = 0;
 2220:   let weightedSamples = 0;
 2221:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
 2222: 
 2223:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
 2224:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
 2225:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
 2226:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
 2227:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
 2228: 
 2229:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
 2230:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
 2231:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
 2232: 
 2233:     matchedSamples += 1;
 2234: 
 2235:     let weight = 1;
 2236: 
 2237:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
 2238:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
 2239:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
 2240:         weight = 1.35;
 2241:         exactDomSamples += 1;
 2242:       }
 2243:     }
 2244: 
 2245:     weightedSamples += weight;
 2246:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
 2247:   }
 2248: 
 2249:   const prob = new Map();
 2250:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
 2251: 
 2252:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2253:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 2254:   }
 2255: 
 2256:   return {
 2257:     prob,
 2258:     samples: matchedSamples,
 2259:     weightedSamples,
 2260:     exactDomSamples,
 2261:     freq: outFreq,
 2262:   };
 2263: }
 2264: 
 2265: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
 2266:   comparableSeq,
 2267:   currentState,
 2268:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2269: ) {
 2270:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
 2271:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
 2272:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 2273: 
 2274:   const freq = new Map();
 2275:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 2276: 
 2277:   if (state.length < 2) {
 2278:     const prob = new Map();
 2279:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
 2280:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
 2281:   }
 2282: 
 2283:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
 2284:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
 2285: 
 2286:   let samples = 0;
 2287: 
 2288:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
 2289:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
 2290:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
 2291:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
 2292: 
 2293:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
 2294:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
 2295:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
 2296: 
 2297:     samples += 1;
 2298:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
 2299:   }
 2300: 
 2301:   const prob = new Map();
 2302:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
 2303: 
 2304:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2305:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
 2306:   }
 2307: 
 2308:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
 2309: }
 2310: 
 2311: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
 2312:   comparableSeq,
 2313:   targetTs,
 2314:   windowSize = 4,
 2315:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2316: ) {
 2317:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
 2318:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
 2319:     targetTs,
 2320:     windowSize
 2321:   );
 2322: 
 2323:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 2324:   const counts = new Map();
 2325:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
 2326: 
 2327:   for (const item of seq) {
 2328:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
 2329:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
 2330:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
 2331:   }
 2332: 
 2333:   const boost = new Map();
 2334:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
 2335: 
 2336:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2337:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
 2338:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
 2339:   }
 2340: 
 2341:   return {
 2342:     boost,
 2343:     counts,
 2344:     windowSize,
 2345:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
 2346:   };
 2347: }
