 2349: /* =========================
 2350:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
 2351: ========================= */
 2352: 
 2353: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
 2354:   lotteryKey,
 2355:   drawsRange,
 2356:   drawLast,
 2357:   drawsToday,
 2358:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2359:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2360:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2361:   topN = 3,
 2362:   targetYmdOverride = "",
 2363:   targetHourOverride = "",
 2364: }) {
 2365:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
 2366:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
 2367:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 2368:   }
 2369: 
 2370:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 2371: 
 2372:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
 2373:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
 2374:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
 2375: 
 2376:   if (
 2377:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
 2378:     !lastH ||
 2379:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
 2380:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
 2381:   ) {
 2382:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 2383:   }
 2384: 
 2385:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
 2386:     lotteryKey,
 2387:     ymd: lastY,
 2388:     hourBucket: lastH,
 2389:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2390:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2391:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2392:   });
 2393: 
 2394:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
 2395:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
 2396: 
 2397:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 2398:     ? forcedTargetY
 2399:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
 2400: 
 2401:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 2402:     ? forcedTargetH
 2403:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
 2404:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
 2405:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
 2406: 
 2407:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
 2408:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 2409:   }
 2410: 
 2411:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
 2412: 
 2413:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
 2414:   const prevPair =
 2415:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
 2416:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
 2417:       : [];
 2418: 
 2419:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
 2420:     drawsRange: list,
 2421:     lotteryKey: key,
 2422:     targetHour: targetH,
 2423:     targetDow,
 2424:     targetDayOfMonth,
 2425:     prevPair,
 2426:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2427:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2428:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2429:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
 2430:   });
 2431: 
 2432:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
 2433:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
 2434:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
 2435:   );
 2436: 
 2437:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
 2438:     lotteryKey,
 2439:     drawsRange: list,
 2440:     targetHour: targetH,
 2441:     targetDow,
 2442:     targetDayOfMonth,
 2443:     prevHour: lastH,
 2444:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2445:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2446:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2447:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2448:   });
 2449: 
 2450:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
 2451:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
 2452:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
 2453: 
 2454:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
 2455:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
 2456:     pTransitionFirst.set(
 2457:       g,
 2458:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
 2459:     );
 2460:   }
 2461: 
 2462:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
 2463:     list,
 2464:     key,
 2465:     targetH,
 2466:     targetDow,
 2467:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2468:   );
 2469:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
 2470: 
 2471:   const structuralPrizePresence = computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
 2472:     list,
 2473:     key,
 2474:     targetH,
 2475:     targetDow,
 2476:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2477:   );
 2478:   const pStructuralPrizePresence = structuralPrizePresence?.prob || new Map();
 2479: 
 2480:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
 2481:     list,
 2482:     key,
 2483:     targetH,
 2484:     targetDow,
 2485:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2486:   );
 2487:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
 2488: 
 2489:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
 2490:     list,
 2491:     key,
 2492:     targetH,
 2493:     targetDow
 2494:   );
 2495: 
 2496:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
 2497:     comparableSeq,
 2498:     targetTs,
 2499:     3
 2500:   );
 2501: 
 2502:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
 2503:     drawsRange: list,
 2504:     lotteryKey: key,
 2505:     targetHour: targetH,
 2506:     targetDow,
 2507:     currentState,
 2508:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
 2509:   });
 2510: 
 2511:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
 2512: 
 2513:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
 2514:     comparableSeq,
 2515:     currentState,
 2516:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2517:   );
 2518:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
 2519: 
 2520:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
 2521:     comparableSeq,
 2522:     targetTs,
 2523:     4,
 2524:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2525:   );
 2526:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
 2527: 
 2528:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
 2529:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
 2530:   );
 2531: 
 2532:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
 2533: 
 2534:   const mode =
 2535:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
 2536:       ? "REPEAT"
 2537:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
 2538:         ? "SPREAD"
 2539:         : "NEUTRAL";
 2540: 
 2541:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
 2542: 
 2543:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
 2544:     list,
 2545:     key,
 2546:     targetH,
 2547:     targetDow,
 2548:     targetTs,
 2549:     8
 2550:   );
 2551:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
 2552: 
 2553:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
 2554:     1,
 2555:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
 2556:   );
 2557:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
 2558:     1,
 2559:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
 2560:   );
 2561:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
 2562:     1,
 2563:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
 2564:   );
 2565: 
 2566:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
 2567:     list,
 2568:     key,
 2569:     targetH,
 2570:     targetDow,
 2571:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
 2572:   );
 2573: 
 2574:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
 2575:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
 2576:   );
 2577:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
 2578:   const maxGapMsBase =
 2579:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
 2580:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
 2581:       : 1;
 2582: 
 2583:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
 2584:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
 2585: 
 2586:   const confidence =
 2587:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
 2588:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
 2589:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
 2590: 
 2591:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
 2592:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
 2593:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
 2594:     memory: weights.memory,
 2595:     recent: weights.recent,
 2596:     structural: weights.structural,
 2597:     late: weights.late,
 2598:   });
 2599: 
 2600:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
 2601:     const grupo = idx + 1;
 2602: 
 2603:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
 2604:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
 2605:     const pST = Number(pStructuralPrizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
 2606:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
 2607:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
 2608:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
 2609:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
 2610:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
 2611: 
 2612:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
 2613:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
 2614:       recentFirst: 0,
 2615:       recentDupDraws: 0,
 2616:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
 2617:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
 2618:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
 2619:     };
 2620: 
 2621:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
 2622:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
 2623:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
 2624:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1PrizePresence, 1);
 2625:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
 2626: 
 2627:     const recentComposite =
 2628:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.52) +
 2629:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.08) +
 2630:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
 2631:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
 2632:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
 2633: 
 2634:     const dominanceScore =
 2635:       (pT * 0.42) +
 2636:       (pSF * 0.26) +
 2637:       (pST * 0.18) +
 2638:       (pD * 0.14);
 2639: 
 2640:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
 2641:     const gapMs =
 2642:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
 2643:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
 2644:         : maxGapMsBase;
 2645: 
 2646:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
 2647:       0,
 2648:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
 2649:     );
 2650: 
 2651:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
 2652:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
 2653:     const isDominantToday =
 2654:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
 2655:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
 2656: 
 2657:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
 2658: 
 2659:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
 2660:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
 2661:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
 2662:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
 2663:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
 2664: 
 2665:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
 2666:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
 2667:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
 2668:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
 2669:     }
 2670: 
 2671:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
 2672:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
 2673:     }
 2674: 
 2675:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
 2676: 
 2677:     const pairSequenceScore =
 2678:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
 2679:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
 2680:         : 0;
 2681: 
 2682:     const structuralBlend =
 2683:       (pSF * 0.45) +
 2684:       (pST * 0.35) +
 2685:       (pD * 0.2);
 2686: 
 2687:     const memoryBlend =
 2688:       (pM * 0.75) +
 2689:       (pSeq * 0.25);
 2690: 
 2691:     const recentBlend =
 2692:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
 2693:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
 2694: 
 2695:     const prob =
 2696:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
 2697:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
 2698:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
 2699:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
 2700:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
 2701:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
 2702: 
 2703:     const score =
 2704:       (prob * 1000) +
 2705:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
 2706:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
 2707:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
 2708:       (confidence * 50);
 2709: 
 2710:     return {
 2711:       grupo,
 2712:       prob,
 2713:       scoreProb: prob,
 2714:       score,
 2715:       probTransition: pT,
 2716:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
 2717:       probStructuralPrizePresence: pST,
 2718:       probMemory: pM,
 2719:       probDuplication: pD,
 2720:       probSeq2: pSeq,
 2721:       probPair: pPair,
 2722:       pairProb: pPair,
 2723:       pairConfidence,
 2724:       repeatBoost,
 2725:       dominanceScore,
 2726:       recentComposite,
 2727:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
 2728:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
 2729:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
 2730:       recentLast1PrizePresence: rm.recentLast1PrizePresence,
 2731:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
 2732:       recentLast3PrizePresence: rm.recentLast3PrizePresence,
 2733:       lateNorm,
 2734:       gapMs,
 2735:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
 2736:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
 2737:       structuralPrizePresenceCount: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
 2738:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
 2739:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
 2740:       dayFreq,
 2741:       dayFirstFreq,
 2742:       isDominantToday,
 2743:       dayFlowConfidence,
 2744:       dayFlowScore,
 2745:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
 2746:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
 2747:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
 2748:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
 2749:       pairSequenceScore,
 2750:     };
 2751:   })
 2752:     .sort((a, b) => {
 2753:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
 2754:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
 2755:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
 2756:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
 2757:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
 2758:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
 2759:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
 2760:       if (b.probStructuralPrizePresence !== a.probStructuralPrizePresence) return b.probStructuralPrizePresence - a.probStructuralPrizePresence;
 2761:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
 2762:     })
 2763:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
 2764: 
 2765: 
 2766: 
 2767:   const reasonsBase = [
 2768:     `Motor: V2_BICHO`,
 2769:     `Regime detectado: ${regime}`,
 2770:     `RepeatRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0).toFixed(2)} | UniqueRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
 2771:     `Pesos: mode=${mode} | transição=${(adjustedWeights.transition * 100).toFixed(0)}% | par=${(adjustedWeights.pair * 100).toFixed(0)}% | memória=${(adjustedWeights.memory * 100).toFixed(0)}% | recência=${(adjustedWeights.recent * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(adjustedWeights.structural * 100).toFixed(0)}% | atraso=${(adjustedWeights.late * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
 2772:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,

 2774:     `Camada condicional: ${chosen?.label || "—"} | amostras=${condSamples}`,
 2775:     `Amostra estrutural 1º=${Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0)} | PRIZE_PRESENCE=${Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0)} | duplicação=${Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0)}`,

 2780:   ];
 2781:

 2783:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
 2784:

 2787:       title:

 2914:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
 2915:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
 2916:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
 2917:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
 2918:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
 2919:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
 2920:         pairConfidence,
 2921:         currentState,
 2922:         targetDow,
 2923:         targetDayOfMonth,
 2924:         prevHour: lastH,
 2925:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 2926:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
 2927:         dayFlowConfidence,
 2928:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
 2929:           key: layer.key,
 2930:           label: layer.label,
 2931:           samples: layer.samples,
 2932:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
 2933:         })),
 2934:       },
 2935:     },
 2936:   };
 2937: }
 2938: 
 2939: 
 2940: /* =========================
 2941:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
 2942: ========================= */
 2943: 
 2944: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
 2945:   const k = Number(key);
 2946:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
 2947:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
 2948: }
 2949: 
 2950: function emptyGroupMap() {
 2951:   const out = new Map();
 2952:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
 2953:   return out;
 2954: }
 2955: 
 2956: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
 2957:   return new Set(
 2958:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
 2959:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
 2960:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
 2961:   );
 2962: }
 2963: 
 2964: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
 2965:   const out = new Map();
 2966:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
 2967:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
 2968:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
 2969:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
 2970: 
 2971:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 2972:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
 2973:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
 2974:   }
 2975: 
 2976:   return out;
 2977: }
 2978: 
 2979: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
 2980:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
 2981:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
 2982:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
 2983: }
 2984: 
 2985: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
 2986:   lotteryKey,
 2987:   drawsRange,
 2988:   drawLast,
 2989:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 2990:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 2991:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 2992:   topN = 3,
 2993:   targetYmdOverride = "",
 2994:   targetHourOverride = "",
 2995:   drawsAlreadySorted = false,
 2996: }) {
 2997:   const sourceList =
 2998:     Array.isArray(drawsRange)
 2999:       ? drawsRange
 3000:       : [];
 3001: 
 3002:   const list = drawsAlreadySorted
 3003:     ? sourceList
 3004:     : sortDrawsAsc(sourceList);
 3005:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
 3006: 
 3007:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
 3008: 
 3009:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
 3010:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
 3011:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
 3012: 
 3013:   if (
 3014:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
 3015:     !lastH ||
 3016:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
 3017:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
 3018:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
 3019:   ) {
 3020:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 3021:   }
 3022: 
 3023:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
 3024:     lotteryKey: key,
 3025:     ymd: lastY,
 3026:     hourBucket: lastH,
 3027:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 3028:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 3029:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 3030:   });
 3031: 
 3032:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
 3033:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
 3034: 
 3035:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 3036:     ? forcedTargetY
 3037:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
 3038: 
 3039:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
 3040:     ? forcedTargetH
 3041:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
 3042:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
 3043:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
 3044:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
 3045: 
 3046:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
 3047:     return { top: [], meta: null };
 3048:   }
 3049: 
 3050:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
 3051:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3052:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3053:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3054:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
 3055:   });
 3056: 
 3057:   const layers = {
 3058:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
 3059:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
 3060:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
 3061:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
 3062:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
 3063:   };
 3064: 
 3065:   for (const d of history) {
 3066:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3067:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3068:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
 3069:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
 3070:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 3071:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
 3072: 
 3073:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
 3074:       layer.samples += 1;
 3075:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
 3076:       for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layer.prizePresence, g, 1);
 3077:     };
 3078: 
 3079:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
 3080:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
 3081:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
 3082:   }
 3083: 
 3084:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
 3085: 
 3086:   for (const prev of history) {
 3087:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
 3088:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
 3089:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
 3090: 
 3091:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
 3092: 
 3093:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
 3094:       lotteryKey: key,
 3095:       ymd: py,
 3096:       hourBucket: ph,
 3097:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 3098:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 3099:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 3100:     });
 3101: 
 3102:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
 3103:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
 3104: 
 3105:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
 3106:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
 3107: 
 3108:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
 3109:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(nextDraw);
 3110: 
 3111:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
 3112:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
 3113:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.transition.prizePresence, g, 1);
 3114:   }
 3115: 
 3116:   const recentComparable = history
 3117:     .filter((d) => {
 3118:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3119:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3120:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
 3121:     })
 3122:     .slice(-20);
 3123: 
 3124:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
 3125:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
 3126:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
 3127: 
 3128:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
 3129:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
 3130:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.recent.prizePresence, g, 1);
 3131:   }
 3132: 
 3133:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
 3134:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
 3135:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
 3136:   const sceneWeight =
 3137:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
 3138:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
 3139: 
 3140: 
 3141:   const activeWeights = {};
 3142:   let totalWeight = 0;
 3143: 
 3144:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
 3145:     const conf =
 3146:       keyLayer === "transition"
 3147:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
 3148:         : keyLayer === "recent"
 3149:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
 3150:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
 3151: 
 3152:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
 3153:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
 3154:     totalWeight += w;
 3155:   }
 3156: 
 3157:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
 3158:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
 3159:     totalWeight = 1;
 3160:   }
 3161: 
 3162:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
 3163:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
 3164:   }
 3165: 
 3166:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
 3167:     const grupo = idx + 1;
 3168: 
 3169:     let scoreProb = 0;
 3170:     const details = {};
 3171: 
 3172:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
 3173:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
 3174:       const pPrizePresence = Number(layerProbability(layer.prizePresence, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
 3175: 
 3176:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.92) + (pPrizePresence * 0.08);
 3177:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
 3178: 
 3179:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
 3180: 
 3181:       details[keyLayer] = {
 3182:         label: layer.label,
 3183:         samples: layer.samples,
 3184:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
 3185:         top3Count: Number(layer.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0),
 3186:         probability: pLayer,
 3187:         weight: w,
 3188:       };
 3189:     }
 3190: 
 3191:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
 3192:     const pScene =
 3193:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
 3194:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
 3195: 
 3196:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
 3197:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
 3198:     }
 3199: 
 3200:     details.scene = {
 3201:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
 3202:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
 3203:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
 3204:       top3Count: 0,
 3205:       probability: pScene,
 3206:       weight: sceneWeight,
 3207:     };
 3208: 
 3209:     return {
 3210:       grupo,
 3211:       scoreProb,
 3212:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
 3213:       details,
 3214:     };
 3215:   })
 3216:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3217:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
 3218:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
 3219:     })
 3220:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
 3221: 
 3222:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
 3223:     ranked,
 3224:     {
 3225:       lotteryKey,
 3226:       targetYmd: targetY,
 3227:       targetHour: targetH,
 3228:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
 3229:       totalDraws: history.length,
 3230:     }
 3231:   );
 3232: 
 3233:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
 3234:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
 3235:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
 3236: 
 3237:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
 3238:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
 3239:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
 3240:       .slice(0, 3);
 3241: 
 3242:     return {
 3243:       rank: idx + 1,
 3244:       title:
 3245:         idx === 0
 3246:           ? "Mais provável"
 3247:           : idx === 1
 3248:             ? "2º mais provável"
 3249:             : "3º mais provável",
 3250:       grupo: x.grupo,
 3251:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
 3252:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
 3253:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
 3254:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
 3255:       lateBonus: 0,
 3256:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
 3257:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
 3258:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
 3259:       freqZeroWhy: "",
 3260:       reasons: [
 3261:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
 3262:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
 3263:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
 3264:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
 3265:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
 3266:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
 3267:         ),
 3268:       ],
 3269:       meta: {
 3270:         trigger: {
 3271:           ymd: lastY,
 3272:           hour: lastH,
 3273:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3274:         },
 3275:         next: {
 3276:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 3277:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
 3278:         },
 3279:         samples: history.length,
 3280:         period: {
 3281:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
 3282:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
 3283:         },
 3284:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3285:         explain: {
 3286:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3287:           targetDow,
 3288:           targetDayOfMonth,
 3289:           prevHour: lastH,
 3290:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3291:           activeWeights,
 3292:           scene: {
 3293:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
 3294:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
 3295:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
 3296:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
 3297:           },
 3298:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
 3299:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
 3300:               k,
 3301:               {
 3302:                 label: v.label,
 3303:                 samples: v.samples,
 3304:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
 3305:               },
 3306:             ])
 3307:           ),
 3308:           details: x.details,
 3309:         },
 3310:       },
 3311:     };
 3312:   });
 3313: 
 3314:   return {
 3315:     top,
 3316:     meta: {
 3317:       trigger: {
 3318:         ymd: lastY,
 3319:         hour: lastH,
 3320:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3321:       },
 3322:       next: {
 3323:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
 3324:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
 3325:       },
 3326:       samples: history.length,
 3327:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3328:       explain: {
 3329:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
 3330:         targetDow,
 3331:         targetDayOfMonth,
 3332:         prevHour: lastH,
 3333:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
 3334:         activeWeights,
 3335:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
 3336:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
 3337:             k,
 3338:             {
 3339:               label: v.label,
 3340:               samples: v.samples,
 3341:               weight: activeWeights[k],
 3342:             },
 3343:           ])
 3344:         ),
 3345:       },
 3346:     },
 3347:   };
 3348: }
 3349: 
 3350: 
 3351: 
 3352: /* =========================
 3353:    TOP3 SCENE READER
 3354: ========================= */
 3355: 
 3356: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
 3357:   const candidates = [
 3358:     prize?.milhar,
 3359:     prize?.milhar4,
 3360:     prize?.numero,
 3361:     prize?.number,
 3362:     prize?.value,
 3363:     prize?.result,
 3364:     prize?.resultado,
 3365:     prize?.premio,
 3366:   ];
 3367: 
 3368:   for (const v of candidates) {
 3369:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
 3370:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
 3371:   }
 3372: 
 3373:   return "";
 3374: }
 3375: 
 3376: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
 3377:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3378:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
 3379: 
 3380:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
 3381:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
 3382: 
 3383:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
 3384:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
 3385: 
 3386:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
 3387: }
 3388: 
 3389: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
 3390:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
 3391: 
 3392:   const rows = prizes
 3393:     .map((p) => {
 3394:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
 3395:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
 3396:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
 3397: 
 3398:       return {
 3399:         pos,
 3400:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
 3401:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
 3402:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
 3403:         grupo,
 3404:       };
 3405:     })
 3406:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
 3407:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
 3408: 
 3409:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
 3410:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
 3411:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
 3412:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
 3413: 
 3414:   const countMap = (arr) => {
 3415:     const m = new Map();
 3416:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
 3417:     return Array.from(m.entries())
 3418:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
 3419:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
 3420:   };
 3421: 
 3422:   return {
 3423:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
 3424:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
 3425:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
 3426:     grupos,
 3427:     dezenas,
 3428:     centenas,
 3429:     milhares,
 3430:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
 3431:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
 3432:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
 3433:     rows,
 3434:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
 3435:   };
 3436: }
 3437: 
 3438: 
 3439: 
 3440: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
 3441:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
 3442: 
 3443:   let score = 0;
 3444:   const details = {};
 3445: 
 3446:   const add = (key, value) => {
 3447:     if (!value) return;
 3448:     score += value;
 3449:     details[key] = value;
 3450:   };
 3451: 
 3452:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
 3453:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
 3454: 
 3455:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
 3456:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
 3457: 
 3458:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
 3459:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
 3460: 
 3461:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
 3462:     add("firstGrupo",45);
 3463: 
 3464:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
 3465:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
 3466:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
 3467:   }
 3468: 
 3469:   add(
 3470:     "groupOverlap",
 3471:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
 3472:   );
 3473: 
 3474:   add(
 3475:     "dezenaOverlap",
 3476:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
 3477:   );
 3478: 
 3479:   add(
 3480:     "centenaOverlap",
 3481:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
 3482:   );
 3483: 
 3484:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3485:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
 3486: 
 3487:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
 3488:     add("repeatExists",8);
 3489: 
 3490:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
 3491:     add("repeatEqual",6);
 3492: 
 3493:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
 3494:     add("signature",60);
 3495: 
 3496:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
 3497:     add("hour",8);
 3498: 
 3499:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
 3500: }
 3501: 
 3502: 
 3503: 
 3504: 
 3505: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
 3506:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
 3507:   if (!currentScene) return [];
 3508: 
 3509:   const ordered = list
 3510:     .map((draw) => ({
 3511:       draw,
 3512:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
 3513:     }))
 3514:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
 3515:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3516:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3517:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3518:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
 3519:     });
 3520: 
 3521:   return ordered
 3522:     .map((item, idx) => {
 3523:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
 3524:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
 3525: 
 3526:       return {
 3527:         draw: item.draw,
 3528:         scene: item.scene,
 3529:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
 3530:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
 3531:         score,
 3532:       };
 3533:     })
 3534:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
 3535:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3536:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
 3537:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3538:       }
 3539: 
 3540:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
 3541:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
 3542:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
 3543:     })
 3544:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
 3545: }
 3546: 
 3547: 
 3548: 
 3549: 
 3550: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
 3551:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
 3552:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
 3553: 
 3554:   const freq = new Map();
 3555:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
 3556: 
 3557:   let totalWeight = 0;
 3558: 
 3559:   for (const item of ranking) {
 3560:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
 3561:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
 3562: 
 3563:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
 3564:     if (score <= 0) continue;
 3565: 
 3566:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
 3567:     totalWeight += score;
 3568:   }
 3569: 
 3570:   const prob = new Map();
 3571:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
 3572:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
 3573:   }
 3574: 
 3575:   return {
 3576:     freq,
 3577:     prob,
 3578:     samples: ranking.length,
 3579:     totalWeight,
 3580:   };
 3581: }

 3583: 
 3584: /* =========================
 3585:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
 3586: ========================= */
 3587: 
 3588: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
 3589:   const g = Number(grupo2);
 3590:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
 3591: 
 3592:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
 3593:   const out = [];
 3594: 
 3595:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
 3596:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
 3597:   }
 3598: 
 3599:   return out.filter(Boolean);
 3600: }
 3601: 
 3602: 
 3603: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
 3604:   rangeDraws,
 3605:   analysisHourBucket,
 3606:   schedule,
 3607:   grupo2,
 3608:   count = 20,
 3609:   targetYmd = "",
 3610: }) {
 3611:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
 3612:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
 3613:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
 3614:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
 3615:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
 3616: 
 3617:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
 3618:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
 3619:     : 20;
 3620: 
 3621:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
 3622: 
 3623:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
 3624:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
 3625:   }
 3626: 
 3627:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
 3628:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
 3629: 
 3630:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
 3631:   const byMilhar = new Map();
 3632: 
 3633:   for (const d of list) {
 3634:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 3635:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 3636:     if (!h) continue;
 3637: 
 3638:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
 3639:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
 3640:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
 3641:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 3642: 
 3643:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
 3644: 
 3645:     for (const prize of ps) {
 3646:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
 3647:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
 3648: 
 3649:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
 3650:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
 3651: 
 3652:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3653:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
 3654: 
 3655:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
 3656:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
 3657: 
 3658:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
 3659: 
 3660:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
 3661:         milhar: m4,
 3662:         dezena: dz,
 3663:         centena: c3,
 3664:         freq: 0,
 3665:         score: 0,
 3666:         targetHits: 0,
 3667:         scheduleHits: 0,
 3668:         sameDowHits: 0,
 3669:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
 3670:         lastTs: 0,
 3671:       };
 3672: 
 3673:       let inc = 1;
 3674: 
 3675:       if (isTargetHour) {
 3676:         inc += 8;
 3677:         current.targetHits += 1;
 3678:       }
 3679: 
 3680:       if (isScheduleHour) {
 3681:         inc += 3;
 3682:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
 3683:       }
 3684: 
 3685:       if (isSameDow) {
 3686:         inc += 2;
 3687:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
 3688:       }
 3689: 
 3690:       if (pos === 1) {
 3691:         inc += 3;
 3692:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
 3693:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
 3694:         inc += 1;
 3695:       }
 3696: 
 3697:       current.freq += 1;
 3698:       current.score += inc;
 3699: 
 3700:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
 3701:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
 3702:       }
 3703: 
 3704:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
 3705:     }
 3706:   }
 3707: 
 3708:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
 3709:     .map((m) => {
 3710:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
 3711:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
 3712:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
 3713:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
 3714:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
 3715:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
 3716: 
 3717:       return {
 3718:         ...m,
 3719:         score:
 3720:           freq * 100 +
 3721:           targetHits * 70 +
 3722:           scheduleHits * 35 +
 3723:           sameDowHits * 25 +
 3724:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
 3725:           recencyBoost * 5,
 3726:       };
 3727:     })
 3728:     .sort((a, b) => {
 3729:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3730:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
 3731:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
 3732:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
 3733:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
 3734:     });
 3735: 
 3736:   const centenaStats = new Map();
 3737: 
 3738:   for (const item of ranked) {
 3739:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
 3740:     if (!centena) continue;
 3741: 
 3742:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
 3743:       centena,
 3744:       freq: 0,
 3745:       score: 0,
 3746:       targetHits: 0,
 3747:       sameDowHits: 0,
 3748:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
 3749:       lastTs: 0,
 3750:     };
 3751: 
 3752:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
 3753:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
 3754:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
 3755:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
 3756:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
 3757:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
 3758: 
 3759:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
 3760:   }
 3761: 
 3762:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
 3763: 
 3764:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
 3765:     centenaScoreMap.set(
 3766:       c.centena,
 3767:       Number(c.score || 0) +
 3768:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
 3769:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
 3770:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
 3771:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
 3772:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
 3773:     );
 3774:   }
 3775: 
 3776:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
 3777:   const slots = [];
 3778: 
 3779:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
 3780:     const candidates = ranked
 3781:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
 3782:       .map((x) => {
 3783:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
 3784:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
 3785:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
 3786:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
 3787: 
 3788:         return {
 3789:           ...x,
 3790:           milhar,
 3791:           prefix,
 3792:           centena,
 3793:           centenaScore,
 3794:           adjustedScore:
 3795:             centenaScore * 2 +
 3796:             Number(x.score || 0) -
 3797:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
 3798:         };
 3799:       });
 3800: 
 3801:     const byCentena = new Map();
 3802: 
 3803:     for (const item of candidates) {
 3804:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
 3805:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
 3806:     }
 3807: 
 3808:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
 3809:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
 3810:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
 3811:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
 3812:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
 3813:           }
 3814:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
 3815:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
 3816:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
 3817:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
 3818:         });
 3819: 
 3820:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
 3821: 
 3822:         return {
 3823:           centena,
 3824:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
 3825:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
 3826:           items: sortedItems,
 3827:         };
 3828:       })
 3829:       .sort((a, b) => {
 3830:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
 3831:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
 3832:         }
 3833:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
 3834:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
 3835:         }
 3836:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
 3837:       });
 3838: 
 3839:     const picked = [];
 3840:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
 3841:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
 3842: 
 3843:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
 3844:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3845:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3846: 
 3847:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
 3848:       if (!item) continue;
 3849: 
 3850:       picked.push(item);
 3851:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
 3852:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
 3853:     }
 3854: 
 3855:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
 3856:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3857:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3858:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
 3859: 
 3860:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
 3861:       if (!item) continue;
 3862: 
 3863:       picked.push(item);
 3864:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
 3865:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
 3866:     }
 3867: 
 3868:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
 3869:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
 3870:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3871: 
 3872:       for (const item of group.items) {
 3873:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
 3874:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
 3875: 
 3876:         picked.push(item);
 3877:       }
 3878:     }
 3879: 
 3880:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
 3881:   }
 3882: 
 3883:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
 3884:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
 3885: 
 3886:     let pushed = 0;
 3887: 
 3888:     for (const item of items) {
 3889:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
 3890:       slots.push({
 3891:         dezena: dz,
 3892:         milhar: item.milhar,
 3893:         score: item.score,
 3894:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
 3895:         centena: item.centena,
 3896:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
 3897:         freq: item.freq,
 3898:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
 3899:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
 3900:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
 3901:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
 3902:       });
 3903:       pushed += 1;
 3904:     }
 3905: 
 3906:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
 3907:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
 3908:       pushed += 1;
 3909:     }
 3910:   }
 3911: 
 3912:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
 3913: 
 3914:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
 3915: }
 3916: 
 3917: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
 3918:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
 3919: }
 3920: 
 3921: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
 3922:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
 3923: }
 3924: 
 3925: /* =========================
 3926:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
 3927: ========================= */
 3928: 
 3929: 
 3930: 
 3931: export function auditTop3Timeline({
 3932:   timeline,
 3933:   lotteryKey = "",
 3934: }) {
 3935:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
 3936: 
 3937:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
 3938:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
 3939: 
 3940:     return (
 3941:       status === "validated" &&
 3942:       Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo))
 3943:     );
 3944:   });
 3945: 
 3946:   function pct(n, d) {
 3947:     if (!d) return 0;
 3948: 
 3949:     return Number(
 3950:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
 3951:     );
 3952:   }
 3953: 
 3954:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
 3955:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
 3956:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
 3957:       : [];
 3958: 
 3959:     const normalized = source
 3960:       .slice(0, 3)
 3961:       .map((grupo) => {
 3962:         const value = Number(grupo);
 3963: 
 3964:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
 3965:           value >= 1 &&
 3966:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
 3967:           ? value
 3968:           : null;
 3969:       });
 3970: 
 3971:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
 3972:       normalized.push(null);
 3973:     }
 3974: 
 3975:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
 3976:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
 3977: 
 3978:       normalized[0] =
 3979:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
 3980:         fallback >= 1 &&
 3981:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
 3982:           ? fallback
 3983:           : null;
 3984:     }
 3985: 
 3986:     return normalized;
 3987:   }
 3988: 
 3989:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
 3990:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
 3991:       .slice(0, 3)
 3992:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
 3993:       .filter(
 3994:         (grupo) =>
 3995:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
 3996:           grupo >= 1 &&
 3997:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
 3998:       );
 3999: 
 4000:     const resultTop3 = normalizeResultTop3(slot);
 4001:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3[0]);
 4002: 
 4003:     const top1Hit =
 4004:       picks.length > 0 &&
 4005:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
 4006:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
 4007: 
 4008:     const top3Hit =
 4009:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
 4010:       picks.some(
 4011:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
 4012:       );
 4013: 
 4014:     const prizePositionHits = resultTop3.map(
 4015:       (grupo) =>
 4016:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&
 4017:         picks.some(
 4018:           (pick) => Number(pick) === Number(grupo)
 4019:         )
 4020:     );
 4021: 
 4022:     const predictionHits = picks.map((grupo) =>
 4023:       resultTop3.some(
 4024:         (result) =>
 4025:           Number.isFinite(Number(result)) &&
 4026:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
 4027:       )
 4028:     );
 4029: 
 4030:     const matchedPrizePositions =
 4031:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
 4032: 
 4033:     const matchedPredictions =
 4034:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
 4035: 
 4036:     return {
 4037:       picks,
 4038:       resultTop3,
 4039:       resultGrupo,
 4040: 
 4041:       top1Hit,
 4042:       top3Hit,
 4043: 
 4044:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
 4045:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
 4046:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
 4047: 
 4048:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
 4049: 
 4050:       matchedPrizePositions,
 4051:       matchedPredictions,
 4052: 
 4053:       prizePositionHits,
 4054:       predictionHits,
 4055:     };
 4056:   }
 4057: 
 4058:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
 4059:     slot,
 4060:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
 4061:   }));
 4062: 
 4063:   function summarize(items) {
 4064:     const summary = {
 4065:       total: items.length,
 4066: 
 4067:       top1Hits: 0,
 4068:       top3Hits: 0,
 4069: 
 4070:       prize1Hits: 0,
 4071:       prize2Hits: 0,
 4072:       prize3Hits: 0,
 4073: 
 4074:       top3PrizeHits: 0,
 4075: 
 4076:       matchedPrizePositions: 0,
 4077:       matchedPredictions: 0,
 4078:     };
 4079: 
 4080:     for (const item of items) {
 4081:       const metrics = item.metrics;
 4082: 
 4083:       if (metrics.top1Hit) summary.top1Hits += 1;
 4084:       if (metrics.top3Hit) summary.top3Hits += 1;
 4085: 
 4086:       if (metrics.prize1Hit) summary.prize1Hits += 1;
 4087:       if (metrics.prize2Hit) summary.prize2Hits += 1;
 4088:       if (metrics.prize3Hit) summary.prize3Hits += 1;
 4089: 
 4090:       if (metrics.top3PrizeHit) {
 4091:         summary.top3PrizeHits += 1;
 4092:       }
 4093: 
 4094:       summary.matchedPrizePositions +=
 4095:         Number(metrics.matchedPrizePositions || 0);
 4096: 
 4097:       summary.matchedPredictions +=
 4098:         Number(metrics.matchedPredictions || 0);
 4099:     }
 4100: 
 4101:     return {
 4102:       ...summary,
 4103: 
 4104:       top1Rate: pct(summary.top1Hits, summary.total),
 4105:       top3Rate: pct(summary.top3Hits, summary.total),
 4106: 
 4107:       prize1Rate: pct(summary.prize1Hits, summary.total),
 4108:       prize2Rate: pct(summary.prize2Hits, summary.total),
 4109:       prize3Rate: pct(summary.prize3Hits, summary.total),
 4110: 
 4111:       top3PrizeRate: pct(
 4112:         summary.top3PrizeHits,
 4113:         summary.total
 4114:       ),
 4115: 
 4116:       averageMatchedPrizePositions: summary.total
 4117:         ? Number(
 4118:             (
 4119:               summary.matchedPrizePositions /
 4120:               summary.total
 4121:             ).toFixed(4)
 4122:           )
 4123:         : 0,
 4124: 
 4125:       averageMatchedPredictions: summary.total
 4126:         ? Number(
 4127:             (
 4128:               summary.matchedPredictions /
 4129:               summary.total
 4130:             ).toFixed(4)
 4131:           )
 4132:         : 0,
 4133:     };
 4134:   }
 4135: 
 4136:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
 4137:     const map = new Map();
 4138: 
 4139:     for (const item of evaluated) {
 4140:       const key = keyFn(item.slot);
 4141: 
 4142:       if (!map.has(key)) {
 4143:         map.set(key, []);
 4144:       }
 4145: 
 4146:       map.get(key).push(item);
 4147:     }
 4148: 
 4149:     return Array.from(map.entries())
 4150:       .map(([key, items]) => ({
 4151:         key,
 4152:         ...summarize(items),
 4153:       }))
 4154:       .sort((a, b) => {
 4155:         if (b.top3PrizeRate !== a.top3PrizeRate) {
 4156:           return b.top3PrizeRate - a.top3PrizeRate;
 4157:         }
 4158: 
 4159:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) {
 4160:           return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
 4161:         }
 4162: 
 4163:         return b.total - a.total;
 4164:       });
 4165:   }
 4166: 
 4167:   const summary = summarize(evaluated);
 4168: 
 4169:   return {
 4170:     lotteryKey,
 4171:     ...summary,
 4172: 
 4173:     byHour: groupBy(
 4174:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")
 4175:     ),
 4176: 
 4177:     byDate: groupBy(
 4178:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")
 4179:     ),
 4180: 
 4181:     rows: evaluated.map(({ slot, metrics }) => ({
 4182:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
 4183:       hour: slot.targetHour,
 4184: 
 4185:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
 4186:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
 4187: 
 4188:       resultGrupo: metrics.resultGrupo,
 4189:       resultTop3: metrics.resultTop3,
 4190: 
 4191:       top3: metrics.picks,
 4192: 
 4193:       top1Hit: metrics.top1Hit,
 4194:       top3Hit: metrics.top3Hit,
 4195: 
 4196:       prize1Hit: metrics.prize1Hit,
 4197:       prize2Hit: metrics.prize2Hit,
 4198:       prize3Hit: metrics.prize3Hit,
 4199: 
 4200:       top3PrizeHit: metrics.top3PrizeHit,
 4201: 
 4202:       matchedPrizePositions:
 4203:         metrics.matchedPrizePositions,
 4204: 
 4205:       matchedPredictions:
 4206:         metrics.matchedPredictions,
 4207: 
 4208:       prizePositionHits:
 4209:         metrics.prizePositionHits,
 4210: 
 4211:       predictionHits:
 4212:         metrics.predictionHits,
 4213: 
 4214:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
 4215:     })),
 4216:   };
 4217: }
 4218: 
 4219: 
 4220: function buildTimelineForDate({
 4221:   ymd,
 4222:   drawsToday,
 4223:   drawsRange,
 4224:   lotteryKey,
 4225:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4226:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4227:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4228: }) {
 4229:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
 4230:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
 4231:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
 4232: 
 4233:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
 4234: 
 4235:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
 4236:     lotteryKey,
 4237:     ymd: targetYmd,
 4238:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4239:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4240:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4241:   });
 4242: 
 4243:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
 4244:     .map(toHourBucket)
 4245:     .filter(Boolean);
 4246: 
 4247:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
 4248: 
 4249:   const daySorted = day
 4250:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
 4251:     .sort((a, b) => {
 4252:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4253:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4254:       return ta - tb;
 4255:     });
 4256: 
 4257:   const rangeSorted = range
 4258:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
 4259:     .sort((a, b) => {
 4260:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4261:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4262:       return ta - tb;
 4263:     });
 4264: 
 4265:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
 4266: 
 4267:   for (const d of daySorted) {
 4268:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 4269:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 4270:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
 4271:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
 4272:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
 4273:   }
 4274: 
 4275: 
 4276:   const chainContextDraws = [];
 4277:   const timeline = [];
 4278:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
 4279: 
 4280:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
 4281:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
 4282:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
 4283: 
 4284: 
 4285:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
 4286:       timeline.push({
 4287:         targetYmd,
 4288:         targetHour: slotHour,
 4289:         baseYmd: "",
 4290:         baseHour: "",
 4291:         top3: [],
 4292:         resultGrupo: null,
 4293:         hit: null,
 4294:         status: "pending",
 4295:       });
 4296:       continue;
 4297:     }
 4298: 
 4299:     let baseDraw = null;
 4300: 
 4301:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
 4302:       .filter((d) => {
 4303:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 4304:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 4305:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 4306:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
 4307:       })
 4308:       .sort((a, b) => {
 4309:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4310:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4311:         return tb - ta;
 4312:       })[0] || null;
 4313: 
 4314:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
 4315: 
 4316:     if (!baseDraw) {
 4317:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
 4318:         .filter((d) => {
 4319:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 4320:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 4321:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 4322:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
 4323:         })
 4324:         .sort((a, b) => {
 4325:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4326:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4327:           return tb - ta;
 4328:         })[0] || null;
 4329: 
 4330:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
 4331:     }
 4332: 
 4333:     if (!baseDraw) {
 4334:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
 4335:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
 4336:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
 4337:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
 4338:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
 4339: 
 4340:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
 4341: 
 4342:         baseDraw = d;
 4343:         break;
 4344:       }
 4345:     }
 4346: 
 4347:     if (!baseDraw) {
 4348:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
 4349:     }
 4350: 
 4351:     if (!baseDraw) {
 4352:       timeline.push({
 4353:         targetYmd,
 4354:         targetHour: slotHour,
 4355:         baseYmd: "",
 4356:         baseHour: "",
 4357:         top3: [],
 4358:         resultGrupo: null,
 4359:         hit: null,
 4360:         status: "pending",
 4361:       });
 4362:       continue;
 4363:     }
 4364: 
 4365:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
 4366:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
 4367:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
 4368:     );
 4369: 
 4370:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
 4371:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4372:         pickDrawYMD(d),
 4373:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4374:       );
 4375:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
 4376:     });
 4377: 
 4378:     const historyStats = (() => {
 4379:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
 4380: 
 4381:       let d30 = 0;
 4382:       let d90 = 0;
 4383:       let d180 = 0;
 4384:       let d365 = 0;
 4385: 
 4386:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
 4387:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
 4388:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
 4389:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
 4390:         );
 4391: 
 4392:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
 4393: 
 4394:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
 4395: 
 4396:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
 4397:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
 4398:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
 4399:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
 4400:       }
 4401: 
 4402:       return {
 4403:         total: usableHistory.length,
 4404:         d30,
 4405:         d90,
 4406:         d180,
 4407:         d365,
 4408:       };
 4409:     })();
 4410: 
 4411:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
 4412:       .filter((d) => {
 4413:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
 4414:           pickDrawYMD(d),
 4415:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
 4416:         );
 4417:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
 4418:       })
 4419:       .sort((a, b) => {
 4420:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
 4421:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
 4422:         return ta - tb;
 4423:       });
 4424: 
 4425:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
 4426:       lotteryKey,
 4427:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
 4428:       drawLast: baseDraw,
 4429:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
 4430:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4431:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4432:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4433:       topN: 3,
 4434:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
 4435:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
 4436:     });
 4437: 
 4438: 
 4439:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
 4440:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
 4441:     const computedForSlot =
 4442:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
 4443: 
 4444:     const top3 =
 4445:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
 4446:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
 4447:         : [];
 4448: 
 4449:     const resultTop3Groups = currentDraw
 4450:       ? getPrizeGroupsByPosition(currentDraw, 3)
 4451:       : [null, null, null];
 4452: 
 4453:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3Groups[0]);
 4454: 
 4455:     const normalizedResultGrupo =
 4456:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
 4457:       resultGrupo >= 1 &&
 4458:       resultGrupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
 4459:         ? resultGrupo
 4460:         : null;
 4461: 
 4462:     const hitPrizePositions = resultTop3Groups.map(
 4463:       (resultGroup) =>
 4464:         Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup)) &&
 4465:         top3.some(
 4466:           (item) =>
 4467:             Number(item?.grupo) ===
 4468:             Number(resultGroup)
 4469:         )
 4470:     );
 4471: 
 4472:     const matchedPrizePositions =
 4473:       hitPrizePositions.filter(Boolean).length;
 4474: 
 4475:     const hit =
 4476:       top3.length > 0 &&
 4477:       resultTop3Groups.some(
 4478:         (resultGroup) =>
 4479:           Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup))
 4480:       )
 4481:         ? matchedPrizePositions > 0
 4482:         : null;
 4483: 
 4484:     timeline.push({
 4485:       targetYmd,
 4486:       targetHour: slotHour,
 4487: 
 4488:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
 4489:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
 4490: 
 4491:       top3,
 4492: 
 4493:       resultGrupo: normalizedResultGrupo,
 4494:       resultTop3Groups,
 4495: 
 4496:       hit,
 4497:       top3PrizeHit: hit,
 4498:       hitPrizePositions,
 4499:       matchedPrizePositions,
 4500: 
 4501:       status: Number.isFinite(
 4502:         Number(normalizedResultGrupo)
 4503:       )
 4504:         ? "validated"
 4505:         : "pending",
 4506: 
 4507:       historyStats,
 4508:     });
 4509: 
 4510:     if (currentDraw) {
 4511:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
 4512:     } else {
 4513:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
 4514:     }
 4515:   }
 4516: 
 4517:   return timeline;
 4518: 
 4519: }
 4520: 
 4521: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
 4522:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
 4523: }
 4524: 
 4525: export function auditTop3Backtest({
 4526:   drawsRange,
 4527:   lotteryKey,
 4528:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4529:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4530:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4531: }) {
 4532:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
 4533:   const dates = Array.from(
 4534:     new Set(
 4535:       range
 4536:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
 4537:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
 4538:     )
 4539:   ).sort();
 4540: 
 4541:   const allTimeline = [];
 4542: 
 4543:   for (const ymd of dates) {
 4544:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
 4545: 
 4546:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
 4547:       ymd,
 4548:       drawsToday,
 4549:       drawsRange: range,
 4550:       lotteryKey,
 4551:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
 4552:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
 4553:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
 4554:     });
 4555: 
 4556:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
 4557:   }
 4558: 
 4559:   return auditTop3Timeline({
 4560:     timeline: allTimeline,
 4561:     lotteryKey,
 4562:   });
 4563: }
